OTHER ICONS OF THE SAME LINE (each one is a separate image; this one must look clearly different from all of them, never the same object again): Additive Manufacturing, Nanofabrication. CAMERA AND COMPOSITION: a single compact subject (or a tight group that reads as one shape), three-quarter view from slightly above (about 20 degrees looking down), centred; the whole subject fits inside a roughly square area in the middle of the canvas, filling about 80% of its height, with a little empty space all around (the game shows it both in a square and in a wide slot). LIGHT: soft studio key light from the upper left, gentle fill from the right, thin rim light separating the silhouette.
